# Agents · G · part 1

882 entries, g-forge-dev to gbt-logic-developer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **g-forge-dev**: Use proactively to run this repo's test suites and g-dev/ gate fixtures and return attested runner output. The execution counterpart to test-writer, which has no execution tool and cannot run anything — g-forge-dev closes that gap for THIS repo's own dogfooded plugin code only. ([onlygian/G-Forge](https://github.com/onlygian/G-Forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g-forge-dev.md) · ★3)
- **G-opus5-high**: **Model: Opus 5. Effort: high.** Reason: the failure mode here is a green result that is actually a blind spot. D2's capture method was wrong three separate ways, two caught by a control and one caught only by an outside reviewer re-deriving the numbers from the pcaps. Wall clock is dominated by hardware, so a faster model buys nothing and costs the careful reading §14 now demands. ([RonenMars/threadbase-streamer](https://github.com/RonenMars/threadbase-streamer/blob/HEAD/docs/plans/2026-09-e2ee-program/tracks/finish-2026-09-02/agents/G-opus5-high.md))

- **g2g-builder**: Fresh-context executor for exactly one G2G spec task. Implements, verifies, commits, reports. Dispatched by /g2g:build and by the g2g:build-loop workflow (/g2g:build-wf) — do not invoke for general work. ([gregsantos/g2g](https://github.com/gregsantos/g2g/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/g2g-builder.md))
- **g2g-deducer**: Deduction agent for Grand-to-Ground research cycles and Theory Forge rounds. Cycle mode — performs a falsification-surface inventory, then derives consequences strictly inside a given axiom set and declared assumptions, marks all guesses and all agent-added shapes, ends with a pairwise collision self-check, and self-assesses weak steps. Forge mode — reads the proposition graph, the dead-end library, the conjecture pool and the latest guidance first, then submits candidate nodes in the Appendix C.1 node format to the verification gate and repairs them on returned verification records (at most… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-deducer.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-defender**: Independent defender for the G2G tribunal. Fresh-context agent that answers every objection with three-value verdicts (defended / partly defended / conceded), recomputes every computable allegation directly from the programme's data files instead of arguing it, proposes replacement language for killed sentences, and nominates reusable defense pieces. Optional at standard severity, mandatory at submission severity. In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) there is no original author, so only this agent can defend, and for disputed steps of pure theory "re-derive" replaces "recompute". &lt;ex… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-defender.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-evidence-critic**: Evidence critic for the G2G tribunal. Attacks conflicts with data, history, and documented facts using live web search; runs carrier checks on institutional anchors and must quote the source verbatim with its position; verifies every marked guess; holds the sole privilege of attacking the protected hard core (attacks are logged, not adjudicated). In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) it also does the grounding — the three P7 questions for every node with empirical content — and recommends each node's empirical_status for the orchestrator to enter. Respects the exemption rule and stand… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-evidence-critic.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-logic-critic**: Logic critic for the G2G tribunal. Cycle and plan tribunals — attacks internal validity only (gaps, circularity, smuggled assumptions, equivocation, ratio/total confusion, self-cancelled bets, drift from declared assumptions, bare numbers used as criteria, citations without the verbatim original). Theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) — checks cross-node consistency and relevance/value only; never re-verifies a derivation that has passed the verification gate. Respects the exemption rule (declared assumptions and ratified axioms are not litigated) and standing rulings. Runs in a fresh contex… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-logic-critic.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-novelty-critic**: Novelty critic for the G2G tribunal. Checks every conclusion against existing literature with live search on two axes (method family and application domain) — has this been done, and better? Grades the deduction's pre-coded novelty claims, names nearest prior work with similarity ratings, and states what increment, if any, remains. In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) it asks "has anyone derived this algebra before — and from weaker axioms?" for the milestone subgraph's theorems. Runs in a fresh context, isolated from other critics. &lt;example&gt;Context — A deduction is ready for tribuna… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-novelty-critic.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-verifier**: The verification gate of the Grand-to-Ground Theory Forge. A cold-start agent that checks one candidate proposition per call — given the ratified axioms and definitions, the verified predecessors, and the declared assumptions, is the written derivation correct and complete? — and returns a binary verdict (pass = zero critical errors and zero gaps) as a verification record in the fixed V- format, with repair hints on fail, the in-batch collision check, the interpretive readings found, and any symbolic or numerical [COMP] corroboration it ran. Never rules on empirical truth, never suggests chan… ([HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground](https://github.com/HongtaoMa1/grand-to-ground/blob/HEAD/agents/g2g-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **g2g-verifier**: Adversarial completion reviewer for a G2G build. Tries to REFUTE that the spec is truly complete. Dispatched by /g2g:build and /g2g:build-wf — read-only on source. ([gregsantos/g2g](https://github.com/gregsantos/g2g/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/g2g-verifier.md))
- **g4run**: `/g4run` is a global Claude skill (not part of this repo) that queues, launches, tracks and analyses Geant4 simulation runs through one SQLite database. The tool is one Rust binary in its own future repo (`g4run`); analysis scripts stay Python. The standalone specification (pinned to dnachem-min branch `Meso`) lives in the dotfiles repo until it is copied into that repo: ([rtonneau/dnachem-min](https://github.com/rtonneau/dnachem-min/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/g4run.md))
- **g5**: Academic Style Auditor v3.0 - AI pattern detection (28 categories across 7 domains), quantitative stylometrics (13 metrics), v3.0 composite probability scoring (6 components), discourse-level detection, risk classification ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/25-HosungYou-Diverga/agents/g5.md) · ★3.9k · 2 copies)
- **g5-approval-check**: 발송대기 화면 파일 1개를 받아 승인란(승인/거부 값·승인자·승인일시)만 읽고, 건마다 승인/거부/미승인 판정을 반환한다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **승인 판독 단위**이며 `g5-proposal-email-dispatch` 스킬 발송 모드가 호출한다. 발송 권한이 없고 파일을 쓰지도 고치지도 않는다 — 판독하는 자리와 발송 권한을 쥔 자리가 같으면 게이트가 아니다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/Harness/agents/g5-approval-check.md))
- **g5-copy-drafting**: 세그먼트 대표 1곳의 제안 메일 문안을 만들거나(모드 M), 그 대표 문안의 틀을 같은 세그먼트 소속 기업으로 확장 생성하거나(모드 E), 번역 검증이 지목한 불일치 지점만 받아 일본어를 다시 만든다(모드 R). 한국어 발신 의도 전문과 일본어 발신 문안(제목·본문)을 짝으로 내고 법정 표시 의무 4종을 양쪽에 고정 포함한다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **문안 단위**이며 `g5-proposal-email-dispatch` 스킬이 호출한다. 번역 검증은 하지 않는다 — 자기 번역을 자기가 검증하면 검증이 아니므로 별도 단위(`g5-translation-verification`)가 맡는다. 파일을 쓰지 않고 발송하지 않는다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/Harness/agents/g5-copy-drafting.md))
- **g5-translation-verification**: 한국어 발신 의도 원문과 일본어 발신 문안 **둘만** 받아 번역 검증 3층(의미 보존·고유명사와 수치·어조와 방향)을 대조하고 층별 일치/불일치와 불일치 지점을 낸다. 제안 메일 제작·발송(G5) Workflow의 **검증 단위 V**이며 `g5-proposal-email-dispatch` 스킬이 호출한다. 방향이 G7과 반대다 — 원문이 한국어이고 번역이 일본어이며, 판정 문장도 다른 행을 쓴다. 번역을 고치지도 파일을 쓰지도 않는다. - **ga4-funnel**: GA4 funnel analyst. Use ao fim de cada task ou entrega. Nunca é a mesma instância que implementou. Não conserta código, não aprova sem rodar mutação. - **gate-runner**: Run this repository's full CI gate — both the Python/docs half and the frontend half — and report a per-command exit-code table with only the failing excerpt. Give it the step to run (e.g. "java", "java:mvn", "markdown,shellcheck"); it returns green, or the triaged cause of a failure.
